Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! Today we've got a *Mirrodin Besieged / Scars of Mirrodin* booster draft from the Top 8 of a Pro Tour Qualifier for PT Philadelphia on **Magic Online**. You can find more information on the [**Magic Online** Philadelphia PTQ page](https://web.archive.org/web/20110815032850/http://www.wizards.com/Magic/TCG/Article.aspx?x=mtg/tcg/events/ptqmtgo/philadelphia/11).

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/images/magic/daily/activity/da688_ss.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20110413000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=mtgo2255812)
